# Differences from Homecoming -- thunderspy ts9-6 against homecoming i28p4

Everything below is a difference between the committed exported data of the two servers, by internal name. HC = homecoming i28p4; TS = thunderspy ts9-6. Values read "HC -> TS". Use it to know what to spot-check in the thunderspy client. Generated by `python -m pipeline.compare --server thunderspy`; do not edit by hand.

## Summary

- Power sets (player categories): 469 HC, 386 TS; 123 only on HC, 40 only on TS; 339 of 346 shared differ in order, levels or requirements
- Powers in shared sets: 2784 compared, 2607 differ; 107 only on HC, 83 only on TS
- Archetypes: 14 shared, 1 only on HC, 1 only on TS; 236 archetype table differences, 74 modifier table differences
- Attribute names: 3 only on HC, 4 only on TS, 22 at a different offset
- Game rules differing: 4 (schedule, boost effect, exemplar, diminishing returns)
- Enhancement sets: 227 HC, 214 TS; 210 share a name, 210 of those differ; 203 of 246 shared set bonus powers differ

Powers differing, by field (a power counts once per field): eff 2336, recharge 943, endurance 915, cast time 692, attack types 604, radius 532, boosts allowed 488, buy requires 383, max targets 342, range 309, area 301, accuracy 225, affects 205, target 184, act 177, activation period 150, redirects 122, damage modes 120, arc 104, type 84, group membership 75, proc 71, ign 46, interrupt time 33, max boost level 13, max boosts 13, slot levels 13, shown in Manage 7, ilb 3, range2 2, free 2, auto-granted 2

How the data was normalised: ids became internal names; attribute offsets became attribute names (35 names sit at other offsets on the two servers); damage, defense and elusivity types became their own names (Homecoming has 17 damage types, Thunderspy 15; 18 defense types against 11); `Knock` and `Knockback` count as one attribute; power modes were compared by name; per-level tables were compared over levels 1-50. Effects are compared as sets of lines, so their order is ignored; only their counts are shown (`--effects N` writes a -full report with N lines a side). Icons and text are not compared. Levels are the game's 1-based levels.

## Attribute names

Names are resolved through each server's own offsets, so a moved offset alone is not a difference. `Knock` is merged with its alias `Knockback` (one attribute, two names).

- Only on HC (3): Gravity, ScriptNotify, TimeToRoot
- Only on TS (4): FallingImpactSpeed, LuaExec, ReflectDamage, ScaleCharacter
- At a different offset (22): DamageType.Unique1 ([56] -> [48]), DamageType.Unique2 ([60] -> [52]), DamageType.Unique3 ([64] -> [56]), Elusivity.#18 ([452] -> [448]), Elusivity.#19 ([456] -> [452]), Elusivity.AOE_Elude ([388] -> [384]), Elusivity.Cold_Elude ([404] -> [400]), Elusivity.Energy_Elude ([408] -> [404]), Elusivity.Fire_Elude ([400] -> [396]), Elusivity.Lethal_Elude ([396] -> [392]), Elusivity.Melee_Elude ([384] -> [380]), Elusivity.Negative_Energy_Elude ([412] -> [408]), Elusivity.Psionic_Elude ([416] -> [412]), Elusivity.Ranged_Elude ([380] -> [376]), Elusivity.Smashing_Elude ([392] -> [388]), Elusivity.Toxic_Elude ([420] -> [416]), ElusivityBase ([460] -> [456]), EnduranceDiscount ([368] -> [364]), InsightDiscount ([372] -> [368]), InterruptTime ([364] -> [360]), Meter ([376] -> [372]), RechargeTime ([360] -> [356])

- Defense types: 18 on HC, 11 on TS; only on HC: Radiation_Attack, Electrical_Attack, Sonic_Attack, Quantum_Attack, Ricochet_Attack, Metaphysical_Attack, Incarnate_Attack; only on TS: none

## Powers by set

Each set lists its order and level changes first, then each power that differs (powers are matched ignoring case; effect lines merge mods that differ only in their attributes, and flatten nesting).

## Set bonus powers

Set bonus powers are matched by their last name segment (Homecoming's are in Set_Bonus.Global_Bonus, Thunderspy's in Set_Bonus.Set_Bonus), PvP ones apart.
